def run(cmd): r = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True) return r.stdout.strip(), r.stderr.strip() sha, _ = run(f'gh api repos/{REPO}/contents/{FILE_PATH} --jq ".sha"') if not sha: print("ERROR: SHA nicht gefunden"); sys.exit(1) out, err = run(f'gh api repos/{REPO}/contents/{FILE_PATH} --method PUT --field message="v4 final" --field content="{CONTENT}" --field sha="{sha}"') if '"size"' in out: size = json.loads(out).get("content", {}).get("size", 0) print(f"ERFOLG! Groesse: {size:,} Bytes") print("creatoros-production-0b29.up.railway.app") else: print("ERROR:", err or out[:200])
